\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I failed because I repeatedly tried to take the cauldron while already holding two items, ignoring the "drop" mechanic required to free up inventory space. I also failed to combine ingredients in the cauldron before attempting to brew, instead just collecting items. Next time, I will strictly follow this sequence: 1) Read the recipe to confirm ingredients. 2) Navigate to the pantry/kitchen to gather ingredients one by one. 3) Before taking a new ingredient, if my inventory is full, immediately drop the least useful item (like the recipe or a collected ingredient) to make space. 4) Once I have the cauldron and at least one ingredient, use the command "> add {[}item{]} to cauldron" (or similar context{-}appropriate action) to combine them, then proceed with the specific wand actions (zulpowand, fairy wand) in the correct order as described in the recipe. 5) Only after all steps are complete, say "> done".} \\
